## Overview

Dr. Pascal M. Jabbour is a neurosurgeon in Philadelphia, Pennsylvania and is affiliated with multiple hospitals in the area, including [Jefferson Torresdale, Bucks and Frankford Hospitals-Jefferson Health](https://health.usnews.com/best-hospitals/area/pa/aria-health-6230054) and [Thomas Jefferson University Hospitals-Jefferson Health](https://health.usnews.com/best-hospitals/area/pa/thomas-jefferson-university-hospital-6230043). He received his medical degree from St Joseph's University Medical School and has been in practice for more than 20 years. Dr. Pascal M. Jabbour accepts Medicare, Aetna, Humana, Cigna, Blue Cross - see other [insurance plans accepted](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/pascal-jabbour-724942#insurance). Dr. Pascal M. Jabbour is highly recommended by patients.

## Specialties

Specialty

### Neurosurgery

Neurosurgeons use minimally invasive surgery to treat brain, nervous system and spinal nerve disorders. Conditions treated by neurosurgeons include brain bleeds, head or spinal cord trauma or tumors.
